(cuda:0), RefinedWeb (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"","newString":"\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on OpenWebText & C4.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: OpenWebText (cuda:0), C4 (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on FineWeb & RefinedWeb.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: FineWeb (cuda:0), RefinedWeb (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  FineWeb (cuda:0) & RefinedWeb (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"fineweb\", \"refinedweb\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"fineweb\": 0, \"refinedweb\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c,      \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} {'-'*20} {'-'*6} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\")\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on OpenWebText & C4.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: OpenWebText (cuda:0), C4 (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"fineweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"sample-10BT\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"HuggingFaceFW/fineweb\", name=\"default\",\n                 split=\"train\", text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"refinedweb\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"tiiuae/falcon-refinedweb\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"content\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n     print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int,   errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"openwebtext\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"Skylion007/openwebtext\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"c4\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"openwebtext\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"Skylion007/openwebtext\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"c4\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\",   if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"openwebtext\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"Skylion007/openwebtext\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"c4\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  FineWeb (cuda:0) & RefinedWeb (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"openwebtext\", \"c4\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"openwebtext\": 0, \"c4\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n        all_reports[ds_name] = ds_reports\n\n    # ── Step 6: Cross-model consistency ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 6: Cross-Model FPR Consistency\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    consistency = {}\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        fprs = {c: r[\"fpr\"] for c, r in ds_reports.items()}\n        vals = list(fprs.values())\n        consistency[ds_name] = {\n            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n        }\n        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n              f\"+/- {consistency[ds_name]['std_fpr']:.4f}  \"\n              f\"(max={consistency[ds_name]['max_fpr']:.4f})\", flush=True)\n        for c, v in fprs.items():\n            print(f\"    {c}: {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    elapsed_total = time.time() - t_start\n\n    results = {\n        \"experiment\": \"wild_data_calibration_002\",\n        \"feature_model\": \"pythia-1.4b\",\n        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n        \"datasets\": {},\n        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n    }\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n            \"n_passages_loaded\": len(wild_texts.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"n_features_extracted\": len(wild_features.get(ds_name, [])),\n            \"reports\": ds_reports,\n        }\n\n    results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"  Saved {results_path}\", flush=True)\n\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            continue\n        feat_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, f\"{ds_name}_features.jsonl\")\n        with open(feat_path, \"w\") as f:\n            for i, feat in enumerate(features):\n                row = {\"sample_id\": i}\n                row.update(feat)\n                f.write(json.dumps(row) + \"\\n\")\n        print(f\"  Saved {feat_path} ({len(features)} rows)\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Final summary table ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  FINAL SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"\\n  {'Dataset':<15s} {'Classifier':<20s} {'N':>6s} \"\n          f\"{'d0%':>8s} {'FPR%':>8s} {'P(d0)':>8s}\")\n    print(f\"  {'-'*15} {'-'*20} {'-'*6} {'-'*8} {'-'*8} {'-'*8}\")\n\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} {clf_name:<20s} {r['n_samples']:>6d} \"\n                  f\"{r['d0_accuracy']:>8.2f} {r['fpr']:>8.2f} \"\n                  f\"{r['mean_prob']['d0']:>8.4f}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Per-depth breakdown:\")\n    for ds_name, ds_reports in The experiment should:\n   - Train 3 RF classifiers from 3 nucleus feature datasets (Pythia, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n   - Extract 19 statistical features from wild text using Pythia-1.4B as reference model\n   - Report d0 accuracy, FPR, per-depth breakdown, and cross-model FPR consistency\n   - Save results to `results/plan_015_wild_data_v2/calibration_results.json`\n   - Update registry and register artifacts when complete\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - Recursive generation depth estimation using RF classifiers on 19 statistical features\n   - Feature extraction with frozen Pythia-1.4B as reference model (perplexity + lexical features)\n   - 4-class depth classification (d0=human, d1-d3=recursively generated)\n   - Wild data should be classified as d0 (FPR = false positive rate of non-d0 predictions)\n   - HuggingFace streaming for large dataset loading\n   - Dual GPU parallel feature extraction using Python threading\n   - AutoDL server with conda environment, network_turbo proxy, SSL cert configuration\n   - datasets v4.8.4 dropped `trust_remote_code` support for loading scripts\n   - FineWeb sample-10BT has too many parquet shards causing unbounded metadata memory usage (~44GB+)\n   - Server: 2x NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition, 1TB RAM\n\n3. computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n51\t\n52\tdef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n53\t    if len(token_ids) < n:\n54\t        return 0.0\n55\t    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n56\t    total = len(ngrams)\n57\t    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n58\t    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n59\t\n60\t\n61\tdef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n62\t    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n63\t    if n_tokens == 0:\n64\t        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n65\t            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n66\t            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n67\t    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n68\t    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n69\t    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n70\t\n71\t    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n72\t        if len(ids) < n:\n73\t            return 0.0\n74\t        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n75\t        c = Counter(ng)\n76\t        total = sum(c.values())\n77\t        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n78\t        return float(entropy(probs))\n79\t\n80\t    return {\n81\t        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n82\t        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n83\t        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n84\t        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n85\t        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n86\t        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n87\t        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n88\t    }\n89\t\n90\t\n91\tdef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n92\t    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n93\t        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n94\t            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n95\t            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n96\t            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n97\t\n98\t    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n99\t    result = {\n100\t        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n101\t        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n102\t        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n103\t        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n104\t        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n105\t        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n106\t        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n107\t        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n108\t        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n109\t    }\n110\t    surprisal = losses_np\n111\t    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n112\t    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n113\t\n114\t    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n115\t        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n116\t        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n117\t        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n118\t        probs = hist * bin_width\n119\t        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n120\t        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n121\t    else:\n122\t        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n123\t\n124\t    return result\n125\t\n126\t\n127\t# ── Data loading ──\n128\t\n129\tdef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n130\t    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n131\t    from datasets import load_dataset\n132\t\n133\t    texts = []\n134\t    skipped = 0\n135\t\n136\t    if dataset_name == \"openwebtext\":\n137\t        configs = [\n138\t         text_field=\"text\"),\n147\t        ]\n148\t    else:\n149\t        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n150\t\n151\t    ds = None\n152\t    text_field = \"text\"\n153\t\n154\t    for cfg in configs:\n155\t        try:\n156\t            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n157\t            if \"name\" in cfg:\n158\t                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n159\t            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n160\t            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", 1\n179\t            continue\n180\t        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n181\t        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n182\t            skipped += 1\n183\t            continue\n184\t        texts.append(text)\n185\t        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n186\t            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n187\t            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n199\t    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n200\t    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n201\t    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n202\t    model.to(device)\n203\t    model.eval()\n204\t\n205\t    features = []\n206\t    t0 = time.time()\n207\t    for i, with torch.no_grad():\n212\t            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n213\t\n214\t        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n215\t        shift_labels = input_ids[:, {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n235\t\n236\t    del model\n237\t    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n238\t    return features\n239\t\n240\t\n241\t# ── RF training & classification e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n255\t    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n256\t    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n257\t    rf = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n264\t    pred = rf.predict(X)\n265\t    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n266\t    n = len(pred)\n267\t    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n268\t    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n269\t    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n270\t    return {\n271\t        \"n_samples\": n,\n272\t        \"d0_accuracy\": wild_features[ds_name] = []\n362\t\n363\t    threads = []\n364\t    gpu_map = {\"openwebtext\": 0, \"c4\": 1}\n365\t    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n366\t        t = consistency[ds_name] = {\n410\t            \"per_classifier\": fprs,\n411\t            \"mean_fpr\": round(float(np.mean(vals)), 4),\n412\t            \"std_fpr\": round(float(np.std(vals)), 4),\n413\t            \"max_fpr\": round(float(np.max(vals)), 4),\n414\t        }\n415\t        print(f\"  {ds_name}: mean_FPR={consistency[ds_name]['mean_fpr']:.4f} \"\n416\t              f\"+/- {v:.4f}%\", flush=True)\n420\t\n421\t    # ── Step 7: Save results ──\n422\t    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n423\t    print(\"  Step 7: Saving Results\")\n424\t    print(\"=\" * 60, \"pythia-1.4b\",\n431\t        \"rf_config\": {\"n_estimators\": 200, \"seed\": args.seed},\n432\t        \"trunc_len\": args.trunc_len,\n433\t        \"n_passages_requested\": args.n_passages,\n434\t        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed_total, 1),\n435\t        \"datasets\": {},\n436\t        \"cross_model_consistency\": consistency,\n437\t        \"classifier_cells\": list(FEATURE_FILES.keys()),\n438\t    }\n439\t\n440\t    for ds_name, ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n441\t        results[\"datasets\"][ds_name] = {\n442\t            \"n_passages_loaded\": results_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"calibration_results.json\")\n448\t    with open(results_path, \"w\") as f:\n449\t        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n450\t    print(f\"  Saved ds_reports in all_reports.items():\n472\t        for clf_name, r in ds_reports.items():\n473\t            print(f\"  {ds_name:<15s} {clf_name:<20s} {r['n_samples']:>6d} \"\n474\t                  f\"{r['d0_accuracy']:>8.2f} {r['fpr']:>8.2f} \"\n475\t                  f\"{r['mean_prob']['d0']:>8.4f}\")\n476\t\n477\t    print(f\"\\n  Per-depth breakdown:\")\n478\t    for ds_name, ds_reports in main()\n492\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"a2ce5c46-8d87-471b-bc3b-e8b0277f0ee9","uuid":"011ddd71-b27c-4502-9134-07a1c07a2e5a","timestamp":"2026-05-16T01:19:04.434Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_2/projects/recursive_gen_depth_est/artifacts/plan_015_wild_data_v2.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration on OpenWebText & C4.\n\nValidates RF depth classifiers on large-scale wild web text.\n- 3 RF classifiers (Pythia nucleus, GPT-2 XL, OLMo)\n- 2 wild datasets: OpenWebText (cuda:0), C4 (cuda:1)\n- Feature extractor: Pythia-1.4B (consistent with training)\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom threading import Thread\nfrom scipy.stats import skew, kurtosis, entropy\nfrom sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestClassifier\nimport torch\nfrom transformers import AutoTokenizer, AutoModelForCausalLM\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HOME\", \"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\")\nos.environ.setdefault(\"HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET\", \"1\")\nfor k in (\"REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE\", \"SSL_CERT_FILE\"):\n    os.environ.setdefault(k, \"/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt\")\n\nFEATURE_NAMES = [\n    \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n    \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n    \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\",\n    \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\", \"trigram_entropy\",\n    \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\",\n]\n\nPROJECT_DIR = \"/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est\"\nMODEL_PATH = \"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"results/plan_015_wild_data_v2\")\n\nFEATURE_FILES = {\n    \"pythia_nucleus\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/features_all.jsonl\"),\n    \"gpt2xl\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/gpt2xl/features.jsonl\"),\n    \"olmo\": os.path.join(PROJECT_DIR, \"data/olmo/features.jsonl\"),\n}\n\nTRUNC_LEN = 507\n\n\n# ── Feature computation (identical to training pipeline) ──\n\ndef compute_repetition(token_ids, n):\n    if len(token_ids) < n:\n        return 0.0\n    ngrams = [tuple(token_ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(token_ids) - n + 1)]\n    total = len(ngrams)\n    unique = len(set(ngrams))\n    return 1.0 - unique / total if total > 0 else 0.0\n\n\ndef compute_lexical_features(token_ids):\n    n_tokens = len(token_ids)\n    if n_tokens == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"type_token_ratio\", \"hapax_ratio\", \"bigram_entropy\",\n            \"trigram_entropy\", \"rep_2gram\", \"rep_3gram\", \"rep_4gram\"]}\n    counts = Counter(token_ids)\n    ttr = len(counts) / n_tokens\n    hapax = sum(1 for v in counts.values() if v == 1) / len(counts) if counts else 0.0\n\n    def ngram_entropy_val(ids, n):\n        if len(ids) < n:\n            return 0.0\n        ng = [tuple(ids[i:i+n]) for i in range(len(ids) - n + 1)]\n        c = Counter(ng)\n        total = sum(c.values())\n        probs = np.array([v / total for v in c.values()])\n        return float(entropy(probs))\n\n    return {\n        \"type_token_ratio\": ttr,\n        \"hapax_ratio\": hapax,\n        \"bigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 2),\n        \"trigram_entropy\": ngram_entropy_val(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_2gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 2),\n        \"rep_3gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 3),\n        \"rep_4gram\": compute_repetition(token_ids, 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_perplexity_features(losses_np):\n    if len(losses_np) == 0:\n        return {k: 0.0 for k in [\n            \"mean_ppl\", \"var_ppl\", \"skewness_ppl\", \"kurtosis_ppl\",\n            \"p10_ppl\", \"p25_ppl\", \"p50_ppl\", \"p75_ppl\", \"p90_ppl\",\n            \"mean_surprisal\", \"var_surprisal\", \"entropy_of_surprisal\"]}\n\n    ppl = np.exp(losses_np)\n    result = {\n        \"mean_ppl\": float(np.mean(ppl)),\n        \"var_ppl\": float(np.var(ppl)),\n        \"skewness_ppl\": float(skew(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 2 else 0.0,\n        \"kurtosis_ppl\": float(kurtosis(ppl)) if len(ppl) > 3 else 0.0,\n        \"p10_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 10)),\n        \"p25_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 25)),\n        \"p50_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 50)),\n        \"p75_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 75)),\n        \"p90_ppl\": float(np.percentile(ppl, 90)),\n    }\n    surprisal = losses_np\n    result[\"mean_surprisal\"] = float(np.mean(surprisal))\n    result[\"var_surprisal\"] = float(np.var(surprisal))\n\n    if len(surprisal) > 1 and surprisal.max() > surprisal.min():\n        n_bins = min(50, max(10, len(surprisal) // 5))\n        hist, _ = np.histogram(surprisal, bins=n_bins, density=True)\n        bin_width = (surprisal.max() - surprisal.min()) / n_bins\n        probs = hist * bin_width\n        probs = probs[probs > 0]\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = float(entropy(probs))\n    else:\n        result[\"entropy_of_surprisal\"] = 0.0\n\n    return result\n\n\n# ── Data loading ──\n\ndef load_wild_texts(dataset_name, n_target, tokenizer, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load passages from HuggingFace streaming, filtering by min length.\"\"\"\n    from datasets import load_dataset\n\n    texts = []\n    skipped = 0\n\n    if dataset_name == \"openwebtext\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"Skylion007/openwebtext\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    elif dataset_name == \"c4\":\n        configs = [\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"validation\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n            dict(path=\"allenai/c4\", name=\"en\", split=\"train\",\n                 text_field=\"text\"),\n        ]\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown dataset: {dataset_name}\")\n\n    ds = None\n    text_field = \"text\"\n\n    for cfg in configs:\n        try:\n            kwargs = dict(streaming=True)\n            if \"name\" in cfg:\n                kwargs[\"name\"] = cfg[\"name\"]\n            print(f\"    Trying {cfg['path']} (config={cfg.get('name', 'default')})...\", flush=True)\n            ds = load_dataset(cfg[\"path\"], split=cfg.get(\"split\", \"train\"), **kwargs)\n            text_field = cfg[\"text_field\"]\n            print(f\"    OK: loaded {cfg['path']}\", flush=True)\n            break\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f\"    Failed: {e}\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n    if ds is None:\n        print(f\"    ERROR: all configs failed for {dataset_name}\", flush=True)\n        return []\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for sample in ds:\n        if len(texts) >= n_target:\n            break\n        text = sample.get(text_field, \"\") or sample.get(\"text\", \"\") or sample.get(\"content\", \"\")\n        if not text or not isinstance(text, str) or len(text) < 200:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        tok_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)\n        if len(tok_ids) < trunc_len:\n            skipped += 1\n            continue\n        texts.append(text)\n        if len(texts) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            print(f\"    {dataset_name}: {len(texts)}/{n_target} \"\n                  f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {dataset_name}: collected {len(texts)} passages \"\n          f\"(skipped {skipped}, {elapsed:.0f}s)\", flush=True)\n    return texts\n\n\n# ── Feature extraction ──\n\ndef extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, trunc_len):\n    \"\"\"Load Pythia-1.4B on GPU and extract 19 features per passage.\"\"\"\n    print(f\"  Loading model on {device}...\", flush=True)\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH, torch_dtype=torch.float16)\n    model.to(device)\n    model.eval()\n\n    features = []\n    t0 = time.time()\n    for i, text in enumerate(texts):\n        token_ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:trunc_len]\n        input_ids = torch.tensor([token_ids], device=device)\n\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(input_ids).logits\n\n        shift_logits = logits[:, :-1, :].contiguous()\n        shift_labels = input_ids[:, 1:].contiguous()\n        losses = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(\n            shift_logits.view(-1, shift_logits.size(-1)),\n            shift_labels.view(-1),\n            reduction=\"none\",\n        )\n        losses_np = losses.cpu().numpy()\n\n        feat = {}\n        feat.update(compute_perplexity_features(losses_np))\n        feat.update(compute_lexical_features(token_ids))\n        features.append(feat)\n\n        if (i + 1) % 500 == 0:\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\n            rate = (i + 1) / elapsed\n            print(f\"    {device}: {i+1}/{len(texts)} ({rate:.1f} samples/s)\", flush=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"    {device}: done {len(features)} samples in {elapsed:.0f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    del model\n    torch.cuda.empty_cache()\n    return features\n\n\n# ── RF training & classification ──\n\ndef load_features_jsonl(filepath):\n    entries = []\n    with open(filepath) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                entries.append(json.loads(line))\n    return entries\n\n\ndef train_rf(entries, n_estimators=200, seed=42):\n    filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n    X = np.array([[e[f] for f in FEATURE_NAMES] for e in filtered])\n    y = np.array([e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered])\n    rf = RandomForestClassifier(n_estimators=n_estimators, random_state=seed, n_jobs=-1)\n    rf.fit(X, y)\n    return rf\n\n\ndef classify_wild(rf, features_list):\n    X = np.array([[f[fname] for fname in FEATURE_NAMES] for f in features_list])\n    pred = rf.predict(X)\n    prob = rf.predict_proba(X)\n    n = len(pred)\n    pred_dist = Counter(int(p) for p in pred)\n    d0_rate = float((pred == 0).sum()) / n * 100\n    fpr = 100.0 - d0_rate\n    return {\n        \"n_samples\": n,\n        \"d0_accuracy\": round(d0_rate, 4),\n        \"fpr\": round(fpr, 4),\n        \"pred_distribution\": {f\"d{d}\": int(pred_dist.get(d, 0)) for d in range(4)},\n        \"pred_pct\": {f\"d{d}\": round(pred_dist.get(d, 0) / n * 100, 4) for d in range(4)},\n        \"mean_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].mean()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n        \"std_prob\": {f\"d{i}\": round(float(prob[:, i].std()), 4) for i in range(min(4, prob.shape[1]))},\n    }\n\n\n# ── Main ──\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--n_passages\", type=int, default=5000)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--trunc_len\", type=int, default=TRUNC_LEN)\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR, exist_ok=True)\n    t_start = time.time()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Plan 015 v2: Wild-Data Calibration\")\n    print(\"  OpenWebText (cuda:0) & C4 (cuda:1)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(f\"  n_passages: {args.n_passages}\")\n    print(f\"  trunc_len:  {args.trunc_len}\")\n    print(f\"  model:      {MODEL_PATH}\")\n    print(f\"  output:     {OUTPUT_DIR}\")\n    n_gpus = torch.cuda.device_count()\n    for i in range(n_gpus):\n        print(f\"  GPU {i}: {torch.cuda.get_device_name(i)}\")\n    sys.stdout.flush()\n\n    # ── Step 1: Tokenizer ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 1: Loading Tokenizer\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\n    print(f\"  vocab_size={tokenizer.vocab_size}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 2: Train 3 RF classifiers ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 2: Training 3 RF Classifiers\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    classifiers = {}\n    for name, fpath in FEATURE_FILES.items():\n        entries = load_features_jsonl(fpath)\n        filtered = [e for e in entries if e[\"depth\"] <= 3]\n        depth_counts = Counter(e[\"depth\"] for e in filtered)\n        rf = train_rf(filtered, n_estimators=200, seed=args.seed)\n        classifiers[name] = rf\n        print(f\"  {name}: {dict(sorted(depth_counts.items()))}, \"\n              f\"{rf.n_estimators} trees\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 3: Load wild data (sequential, network-bound) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 3: Loading Wild Data (HuggingFace streaming)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_texts = {}\n    for ds_name in [\"openwebtext\", \"c4\"]:\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name} ---\", flush=True)\n        wild_texts[ds_name] = load_wild_texts(\n            ds_name, args.n_passages, tokenizer, args.trunc_len)\n\n    # ── Step 4: Extract features (dual GPU parallel) ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 4: Feature Extraction (Dual GPU)\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    wild_features = {}\n    errors = {}\n\n    def run_extraction(ds_name, gpu_id):\n        try:\n            device = f\"cuda:{gpu_id}\"\n            texts = wild_texts[ds_name]\n            if not texts:\n                wild_features[ds_name] = []\n                return\n            feats = extract_features_on_gpu(texts, tokenizer, device, args.trunc_len)\n            wild_features[ds_name] = feats\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n            errors[ds_name] = traceback.format_exc()\n            wild_features[ds_name] = []\n\n    threads = []\n    gpu_map = {\"openwebtext\": 0, \"c4\": 1}\n    for ds_name, gpu_id in gpu_map.items():\n        t = Thread(target=run_extraction, args=(ds_name, gpu_id))\n        t.start()\n        threads.append(t)\n\n    for t in threads:\n        t.join()\n\n    for ds_name, err in errors.items():\n        print(f\"  ERROR in {ds_name}:\\n{err}\", flush=True)\n\n    # ── Step 5: Classification ──\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"  Step 5: Classification Results\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60, flush=True)\n\n    all_reports = {}\n    for ds_name, features in wild_features.items():\n        if not features:\n            print(f\"  Skipping {ds_name} (no features)\", flush=True)\n            continue\n\n        print(f\"\\n  --- {ds_name.upper()} ({len(features)} passages) ---\", flush=True)\n        ds_reports = {}\n        for clf_name, rf in classifiers.items():\n            report = classify_wild(rf, features)\n            report[\"dataset\"] = ds_name\n            report[\"classifier\"] = clf_name\n            ds_reports[clf_name] = report\n            print(f\"    {clf_name:20s}  d0={report['d0_accuracy']:6.2f}%  \"\n                  f\"FPR={report['fpr']:6.2f}%  P(d0)={report['mean_prob']['d0']:.4f}\",\n                  flush=True)\n\n
